- **ambition**: Raise the level of a task before doing it. A meta skill for any kind of work (code, analysis, documents, decisions): it finds the goal behind the goal (altitude), the expert-grade version of the work (depth), and the other fields that can contribute (range), then proposes 2-3 upgraded versions, each with an outside judge, and rewrites the prompt for the one the user picks. Use when the user says "ambition", "be more ambitious", "aim higher", "想大一点", "提高 level", "格局打开", "这个任务还能怎么做", or asks what a top expert would do with a task. Says plainly when a task should just be done as asked. Use when the user wants a task made more ambitious but has not said in which direction. Invoked via /ambition.
